Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple *Scars of Mirrodin* draft from the Top 8 of a Premier Event on **Magic Online**. You can find a full Premier Event schedule [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20101117000000/http://archive.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx?x=mtg/digital/magiconline/calendar).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/mtg/images/daily/activity/da583_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20101117000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=1758282)

### Champion

**ChulioZ, 2-0**
